<main class="wrap stories"><article class="story top" id="story-47340079" data-id="47340079" data-tags="AI" data-score="4148" data-comments="1631" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47340079" data-time="1773257369">
<a class="thumb" href="https://news.ycombinator.com/newsguidelines.html#generated" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/yc500.gif' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='news.ycombinator.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/newsguidelines.html#generated" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Don&#x27;t post generated/AI-edited comments. HN is for conversation between humans</span><span class='zh'>不要发布生成的 AI 编辑评论。HN 仅供人类对话</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New Hacker News guidelines discourage posting comments that are AI-generated or AI-edited.</span><span class='zh'>这条规则强调保持社区讨论的真实人类对话，避免 AI 辅助或编辑的评论影响质感。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New Hacker News guidelines discourage posting comments that are AI-generated or AI-edited. The rule emphasizes preserving authentic human discussion, signal quality, and accountability in community conversations. This matters as AI-assisted writing becomes more common on forums, potentially diluting discourse if not checked.</span><span class='zh'>这条规则强调保持社区讨论的真实人类对话，避免 AI 辅助或编辑的评论影响质感。随着 AI 写作工具日益普及，平台和用户将面临如何维护讨论质量和可追责性的问题。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>4.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>4148</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47340079' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>1631</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-47324054" data-id="47324054" data-tags="Science|Programming" data-score="1997" data-comments="260" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47324054" data-time="1773154216">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">02</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.computationalcomplexity.org/2026/03/tony-hoare-1934-2026.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tony Hoare has died</span><span class='zh'>托尼·霍尔去世</span></a><span class="dom">blog.computationalcomplexity.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Renowned computer scientist Tony Hoare passed away, leaving a lasting legacy in formal methods, algorithm theory, and programming language design.</span><span class='zh'>著名计算机科学家托尼·霍尔去世，正式方法、算法理论和编程语言设计方面的贡献深远。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Renowned computer scientist Tony Hoare passed away, leaving a lasting legacy in formal methods, algorithm theory, and programming language design. His work, including Hoare logic and CSP, underpins modern verification, concurrency theory, and software correctness discussions.</span><span class='zh'>著名计算机科学家托尼·霍尔去世，正式方法、算法理论和编程语言设计方面的贡献深远。他提出的霍尔逻辑和 CSP 等奠定了现代形式化验证、并发理论与软件正确性讨论的基石。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>1997</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47324054' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>260</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-47357042" data-id="47357042" data-tags="Programming|Code" data-score="1518" data-comments="553" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47357042" data-time="1773349270">
<a class="thumb" href="https://gist.github.com/bretonium/291f4388e2de89a43b25c135b44e41f0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=gist.github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>gist.github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">03</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://gist.github.com/bretonium/291f4388e2de89a43b25c135b44e41f0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Shall I implement it? No</span><span class='zh'>要不要自己实现？不</span></a><span class="dom">gist.github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Sometimes the best move is to resist building something from scratch and instead reuse or skip it altogether.</span><span class='zh'>有时最明智的做法不是从零实现某个功能，而是优先复用现成方案或直接放弃该实现。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Sometimes the best move is to resist building something from scratch and instead reuse or skip it altogether.</span><span class='zh'>有时最明智的做法不是从零实现某个功能，而是优先复用现成方案或直接放弃该实现。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>1518</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47357042' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>553</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-47363754" data-id="47363754" data-tags="AI|Privacy|Security|Open Source|Hardware|Energy" data-score="1435" data-comments="343" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47363754" data-time="1773405970">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">04</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.canirun.ai/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Can I run AI locally?</span><span class='zh'>本地能否运行 AI？</span></a><span class="dom">canirun.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Running AI locally is increasingly feasible for certain models, but hardware, energy, and cost trade-offs persist against the convenience of cloud inference.</span><span class='zh'>在本地运行 AI 对某些模型已逐渐变得可行，但与云端推理相比，仍需权衡硬件、能耗与成本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Running AI locally is increasingly feasible for certain models, but hardware, energy, and cost trade-offs persist against the convenience of cloud inference. The piece surveys current open-source options, performance hurdles, and key privacy and security considerations for offline deployment.</span><span class='zh'>在本地运行 AI 对某些模型已逐渐变得可行，但与云端推理相比，仍需权衡硬件、能耗与成本。本文梳理了当前的开源选项、性能瓶颈，以及离线部署的隐私和安全考量。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">05</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://malus.sh" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Malus – Clean Room as a Service</span><span class='zh'>Malus：洁净室即服务</span></a><span class="dom">malus.sh</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Malus offers a ‘clean room as a service’ model to support IP-conscious software development without exposing sensitive assets.</span><span class='zh'>Malus 提供“洁净室即服务”的模式，帮助团队在不暴露敏感资产的前提下进行合规的软件开发。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Malus offers a ‘clean room as a service’ model to support IP-conscious software development without exposing sensitive assets. The approach aims to balance collaboration with IP protection, appealing to teams needing controlled environments for sensitive work.</span><span class='zh'>Malus 提供“洁净室即服务”的模式，帮助团队在不暴露敏感资产的前提下进行合规的软件开发。该方法力图在协作与知识产权保护之间取得平衡，适用于需要受控开发环境的场景。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/upper-up/meta-lobbying-and-other-findings" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Meta Platforms: Lobbying, dark money, and the App Store Accountability Act</span><span class='zh'>Meta 平台：游说、暗钱与 App Store 责任法案</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A repository exposes Meta’s lobbying footprint, including hidden political spending and policy influence around app ecosystem accountability.</span><span class='zh'>一个仓库揭示了 Meta 的游说策略、暗钱与围绕应用生态治理的政策影响力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A repository exposes Meta’s lobbying footprint, including hidden political spending and policy influence around app ecosystem accountability. The findings illustrate how big tech’s financial activity intersects with regulatory scrutiny and platform governance.</span><span class='zh'>一个仓库揭示了 Meta 的游说策略、暗钱与围绕应用生态治理的政策影响力。这些发现揭示了大型科技公司在监管审查与平台治理中的资金活动如何交织。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>1232</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47362528' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>519</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-47367129" data-id="47367129" data-tags="AI" data-score="1171" data-comments="497" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47367129" data-time="1773422341">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">07</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://claude.com/blog/1m-context-ga" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>1M context is now generally available for Opus 4.6 and Sonnet 4.6</span><span class='zh'>Opus 4.6 与 Sonnet 4.6 现已支持 1M 上下文长度</span></a><span class="dom">claude.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>1M context windows are now generally available for Opus 4.6 and Sonnet 4.6, enabling longer context retention and more coherent long-form assistance.</span><span class='zh'>Opus 4.6 和 Sonnet 4.6 现推出 1M 上下文长度，便于处理更长的对话和文档。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>1M context windows are now generally available for Opus 4.6 and Sonnet 4.6, enabling longer context retention and more coherent long-form assistance. This reduces engineering friction around chunking long documents and improves tasks like document QA, summarization, and multi-turn conversations, though it shifts cost and latency considerations.</span><span class='zh'>Opus 4.6 和 Sonnet 4.6 现推出 1M 上下文长度，便于处理更长的对话和文档。长上下文提升了连续性与可用性，减少对文本分块的依赖，但也带来成本和延迟的权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.pv-magazine.com/2025/06/20/ireland-coal-free-ends-coal-power-generation-moneypoint/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ireland shuts last coal plant, becomes 15th coal-free country in Europe (2025)</span><span class='zh'>爱尔兰关闭最后一座煤电厂，成为欧洲第15个无煤国家（2025）</span></a><span class="dom">pv-magazine.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ireland has closed its last coal-fired plant, reaching coal-free status and marking a milestone for Europe’s energy transition.</span><span class='zh'>爱尔兰关闭最后一座煤电厂，正式实现全境无煤化，成为欧洲第15个无煤国家。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ireland has closed its last coal-fired plant, reaching coal-free status and marking a milestone for Europe’s energy transition. The move accelerates renewables adoption and grid resilience challenges; it may influence Europe’s energy market and investment in storage and interconnectors.</span><span class='zh'>爱尔兰关闭最后一座煤电厂，正式实现全境无煤化，成为欧洲第15个无煤国家。此举加速可再生能源占比提升，同时对电网稳定、储能建设与区域互联提出新挑战与机遇。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://agent-safehouse.dev/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Agent Safehouse – macOS-native sandboxing for local agents</span><span class='zh'>Agent Safehouse：macOS 原生本地代理沙箱</span></a><span class="dom">agent-safehouse.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Agent Safehouse provides macOS-native sandboxing for local agents, letting autonomous tasks run in isolated environments without container overhead.</span><span class='zh'>Agent Safehouse 提供了 macOS 原生的本地代理沙箱化，允许自主任务在相对独立的环境中运行而无需容器开销。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Agent Safehouse provides macOS-native sandboxing for local agents, letting autonomous tasks run in isolated environments without container overhead. By evading full containers while maintaining isolation, it reduces cross-agent risk and strengthens security for macOS automation workflows. This approach matters for developers deploying agents on Mac workstations or in Mac-centric stacks.</span><span class='zh'>Agent Safehouse 提供了 macOS 原生的本地代理沙箱化，允许自主任务在相对独立的环境中运行而无需容器开销。通过在不牺牲隔离的前提下提高安全性，它降低了跨代理的风险，提升了 macOS 自动化工作流的安全性。对于在 Mac 环境中部署代理的开发者尤为相关。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>798</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47301085' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>177</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47336989" data-id="47336989" data-tags="Programming" data-score="771" data-comments="257" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47336989" data-time="1773243350">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://bloomberg.github.io/js-blog/post/temporal/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Temporal: The 9-year journey to fix time in JavaScript</span><span class='zh'>Temporal：用九年修复 JavaScript 的时间处理</span></a><span class="dom">bloomberg.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Temporal introduces a robust API for dates, times, and time zones intended to replace Date in JavaScript.</span><span class='zh'>Temporal 提供用于日期、时间和时区的强健 API，旨在替代内置 Date。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Temporal introduces a robust API for dates, times, and time zones intended to replace Date in JavaScript. The article traces nearly a decade of design iterations, trade-offs, and ecosystem debates, underscoring how hard robust time handling is.</span><span class='zh'>Temporal 提供用于日期、时间和时区的强健 API，旨在替代内置 Date。九年的设计迭代与取舍凸显了在时间处理上的复杂性，以及对生态系统的深远影响。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">12</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://agelesslinux.org/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ageless Linux – Software for humans of indeterminate age</span><span class='zh'>Ageless Linux：面向各年龄段的 Linux 系统</span></a><span class="dom">agelesslinux.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ageless Linux aims to be usable by people across a wide range of ages, focusing on human-centered design and accessibility.</span><span class='zh'>Ageless Linux 以以人为本的设计为核心，致力于让不同年龄段的用户都能轻松使用 Linux，并提供友好的界面与文档。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ageless Linux aims to be usable by people across a wide range of ages, focusing on human-centered design and accessibility. It challenges the stereotype that Linux is only for coders by providing a gentler, more inclusive user experience and documentation. The project highlights a trend toward more approachable, age-inclusive open-source software.</span><span class='zh'>Ageless Linux 以以人为本的设计为核心，致力于让不同年龄段的用户都能轻松使用 Linux，并提供友好的界面与文档。此举打破了“Linux 只给程序员用”的刻板印象，体现出开源软件在可访问性与包容性方面的进步。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.grandforksherald.com/news/north-dakota/ai-error-jails-innocent-grandmother-for-months-in-north-dakota-fraud-case" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Innocent woman jailed after being misidentified using AI facial recognition</span><span class='zh'>因AI人脸识别误识而入狱的无辜老妇人</span></a><span class="dom">grandforksherald.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An innocent grandmother spent months in jail after facial recognition wrongly identified her as a fraud suspect.</span><span class='zh'>一位无辜的老妇人在人脸识别错误识别后被关押数月。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An innocent grandmother spent months in jail after facial recognition wrongly identified her as a fraud suspect. The case underscores the high-stakes risk of relying on imperfect AI for law enforcement, where false positives can ruin lives and erode trust. It adds to ongoing debates about accuracy, bias, and accountability in AI-powered policing.</span><span class='zh'>一位无辜的老妇人在人脸识别错误识别后被关押数月。此案凸显在执法中依赖不完美的人工智能所带来的高风险——误判可能摧毁个人生活并侵蚀公众信任。事件推动有关准确性、偏见与对AI警务的问责制度的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://sharif.io/looking-stupid" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Willingness to look stupid</span><span class='zh'>敢于出丑的勇气</span></a><span class="dom">sharif.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Embracing the possibility of looking stupid accelerates learning and innovation.</span><span class='zh'>敢于出丑的勇气可以加速学习和创新。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Embracing the possibility of looking stupid accelerates learning and innovation. It encourages rapid experimentation, honest feedback, and psychological safety in teams. The piece argues that the payoff from taking small risks often outweighs the embarrassment of initial missteps.</span><span class='zh'>敢于出丑的勇气可以加速学习和创新。它促使团队进行快速试错、直率反馈，并提升心理安全感。作者认为从小型风险中获得的收益往往超过初次失误带来的尴尬。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47300329" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ask HN: Please restrict new accounts from posting</span><span class='zh'>Ask HN：请限制新账户发帖</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://geohot.github.io//blog/jekyll/update/2026/03/11/running-69-agents.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Create value for others and don’t worry about the returns</span><span class='zh'>为他人创造价值，别过分担心回报</span></a><span class="dom">geohot.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A mindset shift that values creating real utility for others over chasing immediate financial returns.</span><span class='zh'>强调把为他人创造实用价值放在首位，而不是只追逐短期回报。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A mindset shift that values creating real utility for others over chasing immediate financial returns. The piece urges builders to focus on impact, open collaboration, and sustainable practices rather than ROI alone.</span><span class='zh'>强调把为他人创造实用价值放在首位，而不是只追逐短期回报。文章倡导专注于影响力、开放协作和可持续做法，而非单纯看 ROI。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://hacks.mozilla.org/2026/02/making-webassembly-a-first-class-language-on-the-web/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Making WebAssembly a first-class language on the Web</span><span class='zh'>让 WebAssembly 成为 Web 上的一等语言</span></a><span class="dom">hacks.mozilla.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Efforts to make WebAssembly a first-class language on the Web focus on richer runtime features—like GC, component model, interface types, and better debugging—to let high-level languages interoperate and be authored for the browser without forcing JavaScript as the only host.</span><span class='zh'>将 WebAssembly 打造成 Web 上的一等语言需要补齐运行时功能（如垃圾回收、组件模型、接口类型和更好的调试支持），以便高阶语言能无缝互操作并原生面向浏览器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Efforts to make WebAssembly a first-class language on the Web focus on richer runtime features—like GC, component model, interface types, and better debugging—to let high-level languages interoperate and be authored for the browser without forcing JavaScript as the only host. If successful, this work would broaden language choice for web apps, improve performance portability, and unlock tooling and deployment patterns closer to native languages.</span><span class='zh'>将 WebAssembly 打造成 Web 上的一等语言需要补齐运行时功能（如垃圾回收、组件模型、接口类型和更好的调试支持），以便高阶语言能无缝互操作并原生面向浏览器。若能实现，这将扩展网页的语言选择、提升性能可移植性，并带来更接近本地开发的工具链和部署方式。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Yann LeCun launches a billion-dollar fund to build AI systems that integrate perception, reasoning, and physical-world understanding.</span><span class='zh'>Yann LeCun 领衔的十亿美元基金，目标打造具备感知、推理和物理世界理解能力的 AI 系统。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Yann LeCun launches a billion-dollar fund to build AI systems that integrate perception, reasoning, and physical-world understanding. The effort signals a push toward grounding AI in real-world sensors and physics-based reasoning, with potential robotics implications.</span><span class='zh'>Yann LeCun 领衔的十亿美元基金，目标打造具备感知、推理和物理世界理解能力的 AI 系统。该方向强调把 AI 与现实世界传感与物理规律结合，可能带来机器人等应用的突破。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://daringfireball.net/2026/03/the_macbook_neo" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The MacBook Neo</span><span class='zh'>MacBook Neo</span></a><span class="dom">daringfireball.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An opinion piece examines the concept and implications of a &#x27;MacBook Neo&#x27;—how new design choices, feature trade-offs, and product positioning might reshape Apple&#x27;s laptop line and user expectations.</span><span class='zh'>一篇评论性文章探讨了“MacBook Neo”这一概念及其影响，讨论新的设计取舍和产品定位如何可能重塑苹果笔记本系列与用户预期。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://channelsurfer.tv" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Channel Surfer – Watch YouTube like it’s cable TV</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Channel Surfer — 像看有线电视一样观看 YouTube</span></a><span class="dom">channelsurfer.tv</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Channel Surfer offers a TV-like, channel-based browsing UX for YouTube, making it feel like flipping through channels rather than scrolling feeds.</span><span class='zh'>Channel Surfer 提供了类似有线电视的频道式浏览 UI，让 YouTube 的观看体验像在切换频道，而非刷订阅。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Channel Surfer offers a TV-like, channel-based browsing UX for YouTube, making it feel like flipping through channels rather than scrolling feeds. It emphasizes curated discovery and continuous viewing without the usual YouTube UI clutter. The concept highlights how alternative interfaces can change how users consume long-form video on the web.</span><span class='zh'>Channel Surfer 提供了类似有线电视的频道式浏览 UI，让 YouTube 的观看体验像在切换频道，而非刷订阅。通过更便捷的聚合与连续播放，它去除了常规界面的干扰，强调了对长视频的发现与连贯观看。这个想法展示了替代界面如何改变网络视频的消费方式。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Using Wave Function Collapse to generate hex maps, this guide walks through tile constraints and edge matching for procedural world building.</span><span class='zh'>本文展示如何将波函数坍缩算法应用于六边形网格的程序化地图生成，讲解瓷砖约束与边界匹配的要点。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Using Wave Function Collapse to generate hex maps, this guide walks through tile constraints and edge matching for procedural world building. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://cdn.ca9.uscourts.gov/datastore/memoranda/2026/03/03/25-403.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>US Court of Appeals: TOS may be updated by email, use can imply consent [pdf]</span><span class='zh'>美法院认定可通过邮件更新 TOS，继续使用可视为默示同意</span></a><span class="dom">cdn.ca9.uscourts.gov</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Ninth Circuit says email notices can update terms of service, and continued use after notice can imply consent.</span><span class='zh'>第九巡回法院裁定通过电子邮件通知更新服务条款是有效的，用户在通知后继续使用可构成默示同意。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Ninth Circuit says email notices can update terms of service, and continued use after notice can imply consent. This underscores how electronic notices shape online contracts and the legal weight of user conduct.</span><span class='zh'>第九巡回法院裁定通过电子邮件通知更新服务条款是有效的，用户在通知后继续使用可构成默示同意。这凸显了电子通知在在线合约中的法律效力，以及用户行为所承载的同意。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://davidoks.blog/p/why-the-atm-didnt-kill-bank-teller" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ATMs didn’t kill bank teller jobs, but the iPhone did</span><span class='zh'>ATM 没杀死出纳员工作，但 iPhone 做到了</span></a><span class="dom">davidoks.blog</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A look at how mobile banking and smartphones reshaped consumer behavior and staffing beyond ATM adoption.</span><span class='zh'>探讨移动银行和智能手机如何重塑消费行为与人员配置，超越单纯的 ATM 使用。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.marketscreener.com/news/us-private-credit-defaults-hit-record-9-2-in-2025-fitch-says-ce7e5fd8df8fff2d" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>US private credit defaults hit record 9.2% in 2025, Fitch says</span><span class='zh'>据称2025年美国私募信贷违约率创纪录达到9.2%</span></a><span class="dom">marketscreener.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Fitch reports private-credit defaults reached 9.2% in 2025, signaling stress in leveraged loan markets and potential spillovers to banks via exposures.</span><span class='zh'>费奇称2025年美国私募信贷违约率达到9.2%，显示杠杆贷款市场承压，且敞口可能对银行产生传导效应。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Fitch reports private-credit defaults reached 9.2% in 2025, signaling stress in leveraged loan markets and potential spillovers to banks via exposures. The data underscores risk in non-bank lending sectors and the need for prudent risk management.</span><span class='zh'>费奇称2025年美国私募信贷违约率达到9.2%，显示杠杆贷款市场承压，且敞口可能对银行产生传导效应。该数据凸显非银行放贷领域的风险，并强调审慎的风险管理需求。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.claudecodecamp.com/p/i-m-building-agents-that-run-while-i-sleep" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Agents that run while I sleep</span><span class='zh'>睡梦中运行的智能代理</span></a><span class="dom">claudecodecamp.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author prototypes autonomous, long-running agents that persist across sessions and wake on signals, illustrating challenges in reliability, state management, and safety when agents operate unattended.</span><span class='zh'>作者在原型中实现能跨会话持续运行、收到触发信号便自启动的自治代理，凸显无人值守运行下的可靠性、状态管理与安全性挑战。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author prototypes autonomous, long-running agents that persist across sessions and wake on signals, illustrating challenges in reliability, state management, and safety when agents operate unattended.</span><span class='zh'>作者在原型中实现能跨会话持续运行、收到触发信号便自启动的自治代理，凸显无人值守运行下的可靠性、状态管理与安全性挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.cnbc.com/2026/03/09/oracle-is-building-yesterdays-data-centers-with-tomorrows-debt.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenAI is walking away from expanding its Stargate data center with Oracle</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 正在放弃与 Oracle 扩展 Stargate 数据中心</span></a><span class="dom">cnbc.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI has paused or reversed plans to expand the Stargate data center in collaboration with Oracle, signaling strategic shifts in cloud infra partnerships amid cost and scalability challenges.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 已暂停或取消与 Oracle 的 Stargate 数据中心扩展计划，反映在成本与扩展性挑战下对云端基础设施伙伴关系的战略调整。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI has paused or reversed plans to expand the Stargate data center in collaboration with Oracle, signaling strategic shifts in cloud infra partnerships amid cost and scalability challenges. The move underscores the fragility of massive AI infra deals and hints at potential diversification of compute partners.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 已暂停或取消与 Oracle 的 Stargate 数据中心扩展计划，反映在成本与扩展性挑战下对云端基础设施伙伴关系的战略调整。此举凸显大规模 AI 基础设施投资的脆弱性，并可能促使计算资源伙伴的多元化。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://fortune.com/2026/03/11/iran-war-fuel-crisis-asia-work-from-home-closed-schools-price-caps/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Asian governments roll out 4-day weeks, WFH to solve fuel crisis caused by war</span><span class='zh'>亚洲各国推行四天工作周与在家办公以应对因战争引发的燃料危机</span></a><span class="dom">fortune.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Facing war-induced energy shocks, several Asian governments are trialing four-day workweeks and remote work to curb fuel demand.</span><span class='zh'>在战争造成的能源冲击下，数个亚洲国家试点推行四天工作周与在家办公以削减燃料需求。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Facing war-induced energy shocks, several Asian governments are trialing four-day workweeks and remote work to curb fuel demand. The approach aims to reduce transportation energy use, lower emissions, and cushion households as supply chains adjust, though effects will vary by sector and productivity.</span><span class='zh'>在战争造成的能源冲击下，数个亚洲国家试点推行四天工作周与在家办公以削减燃料需求。此举旨降低交通能源消耗、缓解家庭与企业压力，同时影响不同行业的生产力与执行方式，其成效需实地评估。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.bbc.com/future/article/20260310-wired-headphones-are-better-than-bluetooth" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Wired headphone sales are exploding</span><span class='zh'>有线耳机销量激增</span></a><span class="dom">bbc.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Despite wireless domination, wired headphones are seeing a surge in demand due to audio fidelity, low latency, and reliability.</span><span class='zh'>尽管无线耳机已成主流，市场仍见有线耳机需求激增，原因包括声音保真、低延迟和更可靠的连接。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Despite wireless domination, wired headphones are seeing a surge in demand due to audio fidelity, low latency, and reliability. The shift reflects ongoing tradeoffs between convenience and sound quality for enthusiasts and professionals.</span><span class='zh'>尽管无线耳机已成主流，市场仍见有线耳机需求激增，原因包括声音保真、低延迟和更可靠的连接。文章分析了市场动态、情感诉求以及发烧友和专业用户对有线设备的持续青睐。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="http://satproto.org/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: s@: decentralized social networking over static sites</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：s@：在静态站点上实现去中心化社交网络</span></a><span class="dom">satproto.org</span></h3>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>s@ enables a decentralized social network built on static sites, reducing reliance on server backends while preserving federation. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.adriankrebs.ch/blog/dead-internet/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The dead Internet is not a theory anymore</span><span class='zh'>死网理论不再只是理论</span></a><span class="dom">adriankrebs.ch</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The dead Internet is not just a theory anymore: new signals show pervasive bot activity, content decay, and platform-driven dynamics shaping what we see online.</span><span class='zh'>死网理论不再只是理论：新的信号显示存在广泛的机器人生成内容、内容衰减，以及平台驱动的网络生态变化，正在重塑我们在网上看到的东西。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The dead Internet is not just a theory anymore: new signals show pervasive bot activity, content decay, and platform-driven dynamics shaping what we see online. For builders and researchers, this matters for search quality, trust, and how AI models are trained on the web.</span><span class='zh'>死网理论不再只是理论：新的信号显示存在广泛的机器人生成内容、内容衰减，以及平台驱动的网络生态变化，正在重塑我们在网上看到的东西。这对搜索、信任以及用于训练 AI 模型的网页数据都具有重要影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ziglang.org/devlog/2026/#2026-03-10" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Type resolution redesign, with language changes to taste</span><span class='zh'>类型解析重设计，带来语言风格调整</span></a><span class="dom">ziglang.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Zig language team outlines a redesign of type resolution and related language changes.</span><span class='zh'>Zig 项目组公布了类型解析的重设计以及相关语言变更。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Zig language team outlines a redesign of type resolution and related language changes. The update promises clearer error messages, more predictable compilation, and a smoother developer experience, reflecting the ongoing tension between language ergonomics and performance.</span><span class='zh'>Zig 项目组公布了类型解析的重设计以及相关语言变更。该更新力求让错误信息更清晰、编译过程更可预测，从而提升开发体验，凸显语言设计在易用性与性能之间的权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.thewave.engineer/articles.html/productivity/legos-0002mm-specification-and-its-implications-for-manufacturing-r120/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Lego&#x27;s 0.002mm specification and its implications for manufacturing (2025)</span><span class='zh'>乐高 0.002mm 规格及其对制造业的启示（2025）</span></a><span class="dom">thewave.engineer</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>LEGO&#x27;s astonishingly tight tolerances—on the order of microns—are a core part of why its bricks interlock reliably after decades and across suppliers.</span><span class='zh'>乐高极其严格的公差（精确到微米量级）是其积木几十年后仍能可靠互锁的关键。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>LEGO&#x27;s astonishingly tight tolerances—on the order of microns—are a core part of why its bricks interlock reliably after decades and across suppliers. That level of specification forces disciplined manufacturing, quality control, and supplier coordination, and the analysis shows how tight tolerances can drive product longevity, modularity, and the economics of precision mass production.</span><span class='zh'>乐高极其严格的公差（精确到微米量级）是其积木几十年后仍能可靠互锁的关键。如此精细的规格要求严格的制造与质量控制以及供应商协同，说明高精度公差如何推动产品耐久性、模块化以及精密大规模生产的经济性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://gitlab.redox-os.org/redox-os/redox/-/blob/master/CONTRIBUTING.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Redox OS has adopted a Certificate of Origin policy and a strict no-LLM policy</span><span class='zh'>Redox OS 采纳证书来源政策并严格执行不得使用大语言模型</span></a><span class="dom">gitlab.redox-os.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Redox OS now requires a Certificate of Origin for contributions and bans any use of large language models in development or contributions.</span><span class='zh'>Redox OS 现在要求贡献者提供证书原产地证明，并执行严格的不得使用大语言模型的规定。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Redox OS now requires a Certificate of Origin for contributions and bans any use of large language models in development or contributions. The change tightens provenance and governance, reflecting rising concerns about licensing, data usage, and IP in open-source projects. It may influence how other community-driven systems manage safety, licensing and contributor trust.</span><span class='zh'>Redox OS 现在要求贡献者提供证书原产地证明，并执行严格的不得使用大语言模型的规定。此举强化了贡献链的可追溯性与治理，反映出开源项目中对许可、数据使用与知识产权的日益关注。此政策或影响其他以社区为驱动的系统在安全、许可与贡献信任方面的做法。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://thatshubham.com/blog/news-audit" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The 49MB web page</span><span class='zh'>49MB 的网页</span></a><span class="dom">thatshubham.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The page weighs in at 49MB, serving as a cautionary example of how modern sites can balloon with images, scripts, and third-party resources.</span><span class='zh'>该网页达到 49MB，揭示现代站点如何被图片、脚本和第三方资源膨胀。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The page weighs in at 49MB, serving as a cautionary example of how modern sites can balloon with images, scripts, and third-party resources. The write-up walks through auditing approaches to identify bloated bundles, optimize critical paths, and pare back assets without wrecking UX. Takeaway: page size is a first-class performance lever, and thoughtful auditing matters for both users and developers.</span><span class='zh'>该网页达到 49MB，揭示现代站点如何被图片、脚本和第三方资源膨胀。文中提供审计方法，帮助识别冗余资源、优化关键渲染路径，并在不破坏用户体验的前提下精简资产。要点：页面大小是性能的重要变量，细致的审计对用户和开发者都重要。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://bellard.org/jslinux/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>JSLinux Now Supports x86_64</span><span class='zh'>JSLinux 现已支持 x86_64</span></a><span class="dom">bellard.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>JSLinux, a JavaScript-based emulator, now supports running 64-bit x86 binaries in the browser.</span><span class='zh'>基于 JavaScript 的 JSLinux 现可在浏览器中运行 x86_64 二进制程序，免除本地环境即可测试和调试。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>JSLinux, a JavaScript-based emulator, now supports running 64-bit x86 binaries in the browser. This broadens in-browser testing and exploration of Linux software without native executables, though performance will vary by workload.</span><span class='zh'>基于 JavaScript 的 JSLinux 现可在浏览器中运行 x86_64 二进制程序，免除本地环境即可测试和调试。虽然扩展了可用场景，但实际性能仍受限于浏览器与虚拟化实现。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://lwn.net/SubscriberLink/1061544/125f911834966dd0/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Debian decides not to decide on AI-generated contributions</span><span class='zh'>Debian 决定不就 AI 生成的贡献作出决定</span></a><span class="dom">lwn.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Debian delays policy on AI-generated contributions, leaving patch authorship and licensing rules unresolved.</span><span class='zh'>Debian 延迟就 AI 生成贡献的相关政策，尚未就署名、许可等规则定案。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Debian delays policy on AI-generated contributions, leaving patch authorship and licensing rules unresolved. The move signals ongoing tension between openness, licensing, and automation in community-driven projects.</span><span class='zh'>Debian 延迟就 AI 生成贡献的相关政策，尚未就署名、许可等规则定案。此举反映开源社区在开放性、许可与自动化之间的持续博弈。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/microsoft/BitNet" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>BitNet: Inference framework for 1-bit LLMs</span><span class='zh'>BitNet：用于1位LLM推断的框架</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>BitNet is an inference framework for 1-bit LLMs, enabling smaller memory footprints and lower compute requirements.</span><span class='zh'>BitNet 是一个用于 1 位 LLM 推断的框架，使内存占用和计算需求显著降低。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>BitNet is an inference framework for 1-bit LLMs, enabling smaller memory footprints and lower compute requirements. By quantizing to 1-bit representations, it demonstrates how large language models can run more efficiently, albeit with trade-offs in accuracy.</span><span class='zh'>BitNet 是一个用于 1 位 LLM 推断的框架，使内存占用和计算需求显著降低。通过对权重与激活进行 1 位量化，它展示了在性能受限的场景中运行大语言模型的可行性，同时也存在准确度的权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.markround.com/blog/2026/03/05/returning-to-rails-in-2026/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Returning to Rails in 2026</span><span class='zh'>在 2026 年重新拥抱 Rails</span></a><span class="dom">markround.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Rails remains a viable, productive choice in 2026 as tooling and the ecosystem mature.</span><span class='zh'>到了 2026 年，Rails 仍然是高效的开发选择，生态与工具链日渐成熟。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Rails remains a viable, productive choice in 2026 as tooling and the ecosystem mature. The piece argues that Rails&#x27; conventions, now complemented by modern tooling and frontend patterns, keep it relevant for building maintainable apps quickly. The takeaway is that Rails is still worth considering for new projects on modern stacks.</span><span class='zh'>到了 2026 年，Rails 仍然是高效的开发选择，生态与工具链日渐成熟。文章认为 Rails 的约定优于配置的特性在现今的现代化工具和前端模式下依然有用，能帮助快速构建可维护的应用。结论是，新的项目仍然值得在现代技术栈上考虑 Rails。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://med.stanford.edu/news/all-news/2026/02/universal-vaccine.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Universal vaccine against respiratory infections and allergens</span><span class='zh'>针对呼吸道感染与过敏的通用疫苗取得进展</span></a><span class="dom">med.stanford.edu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Stanford team reports a universal vaccine candidate that targets conserved elements across multiple respiratory pathogens and allergens, showing preclinical promise.</span><span class='zh'>斯坦福团队公布了针对多种呼吸道病原体和过敏原的通用疫苗候选，显示在保守区域的免疫靶点上具有前景。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Stanford team reports a universal vaccine candidate that targets conserved elements across multiple respiratory pathogens and allergens, showing preclinical promise. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://s4dbrd.github.io/posts/how-kernel-anti-cheats-work/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>How kernel anti-cheats work</span><span class='zh'>内核级反作弊工作原理：对现代游戏保护的深入剖析</span></a><span class="dom">s4dbrd.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Kernel-level anti-cheat systems rely on deep integration with the OS to detect and prevent cheating, raising concerns about user privacy and system stability.</span><span class='zh'>内核级反作弊系统通过深度整合操作系统来检测与阻止作弊，引发对用户隐私和系统稳定性的担忧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Kernel-level anti-cheat systems rely on deep integration with the OS to detect and prevent cheating, raising concerns about user privacy and system stability. The post delves into how these protections hook into kernel calls, monitor software behavior, and enforce integrity checks, while noting the ongoing arms race with evasion techniques. For developers, it highlights tradeoffs between security, performance, and user trust.</span><span class='zh'>内核级反作弊系统通过深度整合操作系统来检测与阻止作弊，引发对用户隐私和系统稳定性的担忧。文章解析了如何对内核调用进行挂钩、监控软件行为以及执行完整性校验，并指出与规避技术的对抗仍在持续。对于开发者，这些权衡关系到安全、性能与用户信任。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mitsloan.mit.edu/ideas-made-to-matter/what-happens-when-us-economic-data-becomes-unreliable" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>What happens when US economic data becomes unreliable</span><span class='zh'>美国经济数据不再可靠时会怎样</span></a><span class="dom">mitsloan.mit.edu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>When US macro data loses reliability, policy signals and market forecasts become fuzzier as revisions, sampling biases, and noisy indicators obscure the truth.</span><span class='zh'>美国宏观数据变得不可靠时，修订、抽样偏差和噪声信号让政策信号与市场预测变得模糊。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>When US macro data loses reliability, policy signals and market forecasts become fuzzier as revisions, sampling biases, and noisy indicators obscure the truth. Analysts may lean more on high-frequency proxies and judgment calls, increasing policy uncertainty and asset mispricing. This shift can slow response to turning points and elevate volatility.</span><span class='zh'>美国宏观数据变得不可靠时，修订、抽样偏差和噪声信号让政策信号与市场预测变得模糊。分析师可能转向高频指标与主观判断，导致不确定性上升和资产定价错位。这一转变可能放慢对拐点的反应并提高市场波动。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://codespeak.dev/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Kotlin creator&#x27;s new language: talk to LLMs in specs, not English</span><span class='zh'>Codespeak.dev：用规范而非英语与 LLM 对话的新语言</span></a><span class="dom">codespeak.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Codespeak.dev proposes talking to LLMs in specs rather than plain English, a spec-first approach to prompts that could improve precision and safety.</span><span class='zh'>Codespeak.dev 提议用规范（而非普通英语）与大模型对话，一种以规范为先的提示方式，可能提升精准度与安全性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Codespeak.dev proposes talking to LLMs in specs rather than plain English, a spec-first approach to prompts that could improve precision and safety. The project hints at tooling that compiles user constraints into machine-readable prompts, enabling better reproducibility.</span><span class='zh'>Codespeak.dev 提议用规范（而非普通英语）与大模型对话，一种以规范为先的提示方式，可能提升精准度与安全性。该项目暗示将用户约束编译为机器可读的提示，以提高可重复性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://sitespy.app" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: I built a tool that watches webpages and exposes changes as RSS</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：我做了一个监视网页并把变化曝露为 RSS 的工具</span></a><span class="dom">sitespy.app</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This tool watches web pages for changes and exposes detected updates as RSS feeds, letting users integrate page monitoring into existing feed readers and automation pipelines.</span><span class='zh'>该工具监视网页变化并将检测到的更新以 RSS 形式输出，便于用户在现有阅读器或自动化流程中集成监控。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This tool watches web pages for changes and exposes detected updates as RSS feeds, letting users integrate page monitoring into existing feed readers and automation pipelines. It&#x27;s a lightweight solution for tracking content that lacks native feeds or APIs, useful for alerts, scraping alternatives, and simple change detection.</span><span class='zh'>该工具监视网页变化并将检测到的更新以 RSS 形式输出，便于用户在现有阅读器或自动化流程中集成监控。它是跟踪缺少原生订阅或 API 的内容的轻量方案，适合用于警报、替代抓取及简单变更检测。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>RISC-V performance and ecosystem maturity face scrutiny as real-world workloads lag behind established ISAs like x86 and ARM.</span><span class='zh'>在真实工作负载下，RISC-V 的性能与生态成熟度被质疑，与 x86/ARM 等成熟生态相比仍存在差距。文章聚焦工具链、编译器与硬件成熟度的不平衡对实际吞吐量和普及的影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/knowsuchagency/mcp2cli" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Mcp2cli – One CLI for every API, 96-99% fewer tokens than native MCP</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Mcp2cli——一条命令行即可对接所有 API，令牌消耗比原生 MCP 少 96–99%</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>McP2cli abstracts multiple APIs behind a single CLI interface, enabling developers to call diverse services with fewer tokens.</span><span class='zh'>Mcp2cli 将多种 API 封装在一个统一的命令行接口下，开发者可用更少的 token 调用各类服务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>McP2cli abstracts multiple APIs behind a single CLI interface, enabling developers to call diverse services with fewer tokens. The project promises dramatic token savings and a smoother developer experience, potentially accelerating automation workflows. It illustrates a trend toward universal CLI layers for heterogeneous APIs.</span><span class='zh'>Mcp2cli 将多种 API 封装在一个统一的命令行接口下，开发者可用更少的 token 调用各类服务。该项目承诺显著的令牌节省和更顺畅的开发体验，可能加速自动化工作流，体现了对异构 API 的通用 CLI 层的趋势。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DenchClaw is a local CRM built on top of OpenClaw, prioritizing offline-first operation and privacy-preserving data storage.</span><span class='zh'>DenchClaw 是一个基于 OpenClaw 的本地 CRM，强调离线优先与隐私友好的数据存储。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DenchClaw is a local CRM built on top of OpenClaw, prioritizing offline-first operation and privacy-preserving data storage. It demonstrates how open-source CRM tooling can be extended with local-first design, enabling small teams to manage customers without relying on cloud services.</span><span class='zh'>DenchClaw 是一个基于 OpenClaw 的本地 CRM，强调离线优先与隐私友好的数据存储。它展示了开源 CRM 工具如何通过本地优先设计扩展，让小型团队能够在不依赖云端的情况下高效管理客户。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/gritzko/librdx/tree/master/be" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Beagle, a source code management system that stores AST trees</span><span class='zh'>Beagle：存储 AST 树的源代码管理系统</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Beagle is a source-code management system that stores abstract syntax trees (ASTs) instead of plain text.</span><span class='zh'>Beagle 是一个以存储抽象语法树（AST）为核心的源代码管理系统，而非传统的文本存储。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Beagle is a source-code management system that stores abstract syntax trees (ASTs) instead of plain text. This enables language-aware diffs, safer refactoring, and cross-language tooling by operating on structural code representations. The approach promises deeper code intelligence than traditional diff-based VCS.</span><span class='zh'>Beagle 是一个以存储抽象语法树（AST）为核心的源代码管理系统，而非传统的文本存储。通过对代码结构的操作，可以实现语言感知的差异比较、更加安全的重构，以及跨语言的工具链协作。该方法在传统基于文本的版本控制之上带来更深层次的代码智能。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Terminal emulators can support clickable links via OSC 8, enabling direct navigation from CLI output. This capability improves developer workflows by making URLs and references actionable inside terminals. The note covers practical adoption, portability caveats, and user expectations.</span><span class='zh'>通过 OSC 8，终端模拟器可以实现可点击的链接，使 CLI 输出中的 URL 能直接跳转。此能力能提升开发工作流的效率与可用性，但在跨终端的可移植性与一致性方面也需注意。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Compresr-ai/Context-Gateway" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Context Gateway – Compress agent context before it hits the LLM</span><span class='zh'>Context Gateway：在进入 LLM 之前对上下文进行压缩</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Context Gateway introduces a pre-processing layer that compresses or summarizes agent context before sending prompts to an LLM, reducing token usage and latency.</span><span class='zh'>Context Gateway 在将上下文传给大型语言模型之前先进行压缩/摘要处理，减少令牌用量和延迟。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Context Gateway introduces a pre-processing layer that compresses or summarizes agent context before sending prompts to an LLM, reducing token usage and latency. The approach can lower costs and improve privacy by trimming unnecessary information. It&#x27;s a practical lever for scalable AI assistants.</span><span class='zh'>Context Gateway 在将上下文传给大型语言模型之前先进行压缩/摘要处理，减少令牌用量和延迟。此方法有助于降低成本、提升隐私保护，并提升对话的可扩展性。为可扩展的 AI 助手提供了一个实用的优化点。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/manifold-systems/manifold/blob/master/docs/articles/binding_exprs.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Rethinking Syntax: Binding by Adjacency</span><span class='zh'>重新思考语法：按相邻绑定</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A proposal to bind symbols by their adjacency in code rather than strict lexical or lexical scope.</span><span class='zh'>提出按代码临近性来绑定标识符，而非按严格作用域。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A proposal to bind symbols by their adjacency in code rather than strict lexical or lexical scope. The approach could simplify DSL design, improve composability, and affect compiler and analyzer implementations.</span><span class='zh'>提出按代码临近性来绑定标识符，而非按严格作用域。此思路或简化领域特定语言的设计、提升模块化，并影响编译器与静态分析实现。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/RightNow-AI/autokernel" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AutoKernel: Autoresearch for GPU Kernels</span><span class='zh'>AutoKernel：针对 GPU 内核的自动化研究工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AutoKernel automates search and tuning of GPU kernels, using autoresearch techniques to discover high‑performance implementations without manual CUDA optimization.</span><span class='zh'>AutoKernel 通过自动化搜索和调优 GPU 内核，利用自研究方法发现高性能实现，减少手工 CUDA 优化需求。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AutoKernel automates search and tuning of GPU kernels, using autoresearch techniques to discover high‑performance implementations without manual CUDA optimization. This can accelerate performance engineering and help developers get closer to peak hardware efficiency with less bespoke effort.</span><span class='zh'>AutoKernel 通过自动化搜索和调优 GPU 内核，利用自研究方法发现高性能实现，减少手工 CUDA 优化需求。该工具能加快性能工程工作，帮助开发者以更少定制化投入靠近硬件峰值效率。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>CSMWrap enables legacy BIOS booting on UEFI-only systems using SeaBIOS, helping compatibility for older operating systems or bootloaders.</span><span class='zh'>CSMWrap 通过 SeaBIOS 使在仅有 UEFI 的系统上实现传统 BIOS 引导成为可能，提升对旧系统或引导器的兼容性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>CSMWrap enables legacy BIOS booting on UEFI-only systems using SeaBIOS, helping compatibility for older operating systems or bootloaders.
